### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは届いたデータの順序と到達を保証し、UDPは保証せず、そのぶん速くて軽いです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar o limpiar tu propia rama antes de compartirla, porque deja un historial lineal. Usa merge para integrar una rama que otros ya usan, porque no reescribe commits publicados.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠传输，保证数据送达且有序；UDP 不保证送达，但没有连接和重传的开销，所以更快、延迟更低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is yours alone and you want a clean, linear history, like pulling the latest main into your feature branch before opening a PR.

Merge when others already work on the branch or you want the history to show exactly when branches came together, since rebasing rewrites commits that others may have built on.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database before it's applied to the main data files. If the system crashes mid-write, the database replays that log on restart to recover exactly what was committed, with nothing lost or half-applied.
```

(function(){
  var KEY = 'beijing-trip';
  var S = {days: []}, selDay = 0, editing = null;
  var $ = function(id){ return document.g …  it.name;
    $('amt').value = String(it.amount);
    $('addbox').open = true;
    render();
    $('name').focus();

  $('save').onclick = function(){
    var name = $('name').value.trim(), amt = parseFloat($('amt').value);
    if (!name || isNaN(amt) || amt < 0) return;
    var item = {name: name, amount: Math.round(amt * 100) / 100};
    if (editing) S.days[editing.d].items.splice(editing.i, 1);
    S.days[selDay].items.push(item);
    editing = null;
    $('name').value = ''; $('amt').value = '';
    persist();
    $('name').focus();
  };

  $('cancel').onclick = reset;

  $('del').onclick = function(){
    if (!editing) return;
    S.days[editing.d].items.splice(editing.i, 1);
    editing = null;
    $('name').value = ''; $('amt').value = '';
    persist();
  };

  $('addDay').onclick = function(){
    var last = S.days[S.days.length - 1];
    S.days.push({date: last ? nextIso(last.date) : iso(new Date()), items: []});
    selDay = S.days.length - 1;
    persist();
  };

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(init);
  ducter.onChange(function(){
    if (editing) return;
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(function(v){ if (!editing) init(v); });
  });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

      const d = addDays(now, -i);
      const k = key(d);
      const b = document.createElement('button');
      b.className = 'hb-day' + (i === 0 ? ' hb-now' : '');
      b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(!!log[k]));
      b.setAttribute('aria-label', h.name + ' on ' + k);
      b.title = k;
      b.onclick = () => toggle(h.id, k);
      days.appendChild(b);
    }

    const hint = document.createElement('div');
    hint.className = 'hb-hint';
    hint.textContent = 'Last 14 days. Tap a day to fix it.';

    row.append(top, days, hint);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });

  todayEl.textContent = done + ' / ' + HABITS.length;
  bestEl.textContent = best > 0 ? 'Best current streak: ' + best + ' days' : 'Tick a habit to start a streak';
  ducter.summary(done + ' / ' + HABITS.length + ' done today');
}

(async function init() {
  data = (await ducter.get('habitLog')) || {};
  render();
  ducter.onChange(async () => {
    data = (await ducter.get('habitLog')) || {};
    render();
  });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"habits":[{"id":"reading","name":"Reading"},{"id":"run","name":"A run"},{"id":"nophone","name":"No phone after 11"}]}

};

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Here's a page with the four waiting PRs. Tap each one when it lands, and the count drops. It shows the status as of 10:20 and won't update on its own.
  [an app card under the message: made "Waiting PRs", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1637 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub">Tick each one off when it lands</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">PRs waiting on someone</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
  <p class="k-sum-sub" style="margin-top:12px">Status as of Sat 10:20.</p>
</main>
<script>
const PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
let landed = {};

function render() {
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  let open = 0;
  PRS.forEach(function (pr) {
    const isDone = !!landed[pr.id];
    if (!isDone) open++;
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    row.style.cursor = "pointer";
    row.style.opacity = isDone ? "0.5" : "1";
    row.innerHTML =
      '<div class="k-row-main">' +
        '<div style="font-weight:600;text-decoration:' + (isDone ? "line-through" : "none") + '">' +
          (isDone ? "☑ " : "☐ ") + pr.title + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + pr.repo + '</div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value" style="font-size:12px">' + (isDone ? "Landed" : pr.who) + '</div>';
    row.onclick = function () {
      landed[pr.id] = !landed[pr.id];
      if (!landed[pr.id]) delete landed[pr.id];
      ducter.set("landed", landed);
      render();
    };
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = open;
  ducter.summary(open + " waiting");
}

Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(function (v) {
  landed = v || {};
  render();
});
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"Compaction cut","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #412","who":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"Retry on 502","repo":"nowledge-co/mem #10287","who":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","who":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"Apps bar","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #418","who":"Draft, not yet waiting on anyone"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

$("list").onclick=e=>{

  if(d){const xs=items();xs.splice(+d.dataset.del,1);ducter.set("items",xs);draw();return}
  const r=e.target.closest(".k-row");
  if(r)r.classList.toggle("open");
};
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=parseFloat($("amt").value);
  if(!amt)return;
  const xs=items();
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value.trim()||"北京出差 11/20–21",day,cat,amt});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  $("amt").value="";
  $("add").open=false;
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
drawForm();
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥→北京南","from":"你的预订"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店(望京) 11/20–21 两晚","from":"你的预订"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让做 TTL 那个任务再补一条正好到期那一刻的测试，补好告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户说："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

在现有假时钟的基础上，再加一条测试，覆盖 TTL 恰好到期的那一刻：
- 时间推进到刚好等于 TTL 的时刻，断言此时条目已过期（取不到值）。
- 时间比到期早一个最小单位（例如 1 毫秒）时，断言条目仍然有效。
- 只改测试，不动生产代码，除非发现真实 bug，发现的话先告诉我再改。

完成标准：新测试通过，并把测试输出和同一套测试连续跑多次的结果报告回来。
范围外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR，留在当前分支提交即可。
```

--- step 1: user says: Is pnpm actually faster than npm for a big monorepo? One line.
  [partner message] Usually yes: pnpm's shared content store and hard links make installs faster and use less disk in big monorepos, though npm 7+ has narrowed the gap and build time often matters more than install time.
```
